package com.aboezzat.vpn.proxy

import java.io.BufferedInputStream
import java.io.BufferedOutputStream
import java.net.ServerSocket
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference
import kotlin.concurrent.thread
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class ProxyTcpTunnelTest {
    @Test
    fun opensHttpConnectTunnelAndReturnsUsableSocket() {
        val server = ServerSocket(0)
        val requestValid = AtomicBoolean(false)
        val serverFailure = AtomicReference<Throwable?>(null)
        val worker = thread(start = true) {
            try {
                server.accept().use { peer ->
                    peer.soTimeout = 3000
                    val input = BufferedInputStream(peer.getInputStream())
                    val request = readHttpHeaders(input)
                    requestValid.set(
                        request.startsWith("CONNECT example.com:443 HTTP/1.1\r\n") &&
                            request.contains("Proxy-Authorization: Basic dXNlcjpwYXNz\r\n")
                    )
                    val output = BufferedOutputStream(peer.getOutputStream())
                    output.write("HTTP/1.1 200 Connection established\r\n\r\n".toByteArray(Charsets.US_ASCII))
                    output.flush()
                    val payload = ByteArray(4)
                    input.readFully(payload)
                    assertEquals("ping", String(payload, Charsets.US_ASCII))
                    output.write("pong".toByteArray(Charsets.US_ASCII))
                    output.flush()
                }
            } catch (error: Throwable) {
                serverFailure.set(error)
            }
        }

        try {
            val protectedBeforeConnect = AtomicBoolean(false)
            val tunnel = ProxyTcpTunnel.open(
                profile = ProxyProfile(
                    "HTTP test", "127.0.0.1", server.localPort, ProxyProtocol.HTTP_CONNECT,
                    username = "user", password = "pass"
                ),
                destinationHost = "example.com",
                destinationPort = 443,
                protectSocket = { socket ->
                    protectedBeforeConnect.set(!socket.isConnected)
                    true
                }
            )
            tunnel.use { socket ->
                assertEquals("A live tunnel must not retain the handshake read timeout", 0, socket.soTimeout)
                socket.getOutputStream().write("ping".toByteArray(Charsets.US_ASCII))
                val response = ByteArray(4)
                socket.getInputStream().readFully(response)
                assertEquals("pong", String(response, Charsets.US_ASCII))
            }
            worker.join(3000)
            assertTrue("protect() callback must run before connect", protectedBeforeConnect.get())
            assertTrue("HTTP CONNECT request must include destination and auth", requestValid.get())
            serverFailure.get()?.let { throw AssertionError("Mock HTTP proxy failed", it) }
        } finally {
            server.close()
            worker.join(1000)
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun opensSocks5AuthenticatedRemoteDnsTunnel() {
        val server = ServerSocket(0)
        val requestValid = AtomicBoolean(false)
        val serverFailure = AtomicReference<Throwable?>(null)
        val worker = thread(start = true) {
            try {
                server.accept().use { peer ->
                    peer.soTimeout = 3000
                    val input = BufferedInputStream(peer.getInputStream())
                    val output = BufferedOutputStream(peer.getOutputStream())

                    assertEquals(listOf(5, 2, 0, 2), readExactly(input, 4).map { it.toInt() and 0xff })
                    output.write(byteArrayOf(5, 2))
                    output.flush()

                    val authVersion = input.read()
                    val usernameLength = input.read()
                    val username = String(readExactly(input, usernameLength), Charsets.UTF_8)
                    val passwordLength = input.read()
                    val password = String(readExactly(input, passwordLength), Charsets.UTF_8)
                    assertTrue(authVersion == 1 && username == "user" && password == "pass")
                    output.write(byteArrayOf(1, 0))
                    output.flush()

                    val header = readExactly(input, 4)
                    assertEquals(5, header[0].toInt() and 0xff)
                    assertEquals(1, header[1].toInt() and 0xff)
                    assertEquals(3, header[3].toInt() and 0xff)
                    val hostLength = input.read()
                    val host = String(readExactly(input, hostLength), Charsets.US_ASCII)
                    val port = readExactly(input, 2).map { it.toInt() and 0xff }
                    requestValid.set(host == "example.com" && port == listOf(1, 187))

                    output.write(byteArrayOf(5, 0, 0, 1, 127, 0, 0, 1, 0, 80))
                    output.flush()
                    val payload = ByteArray(4)
                    input.readFully(payload)
                    assertEquals("ping", String(payload, Charsets.US_ASCII))
                    output.write("pong".toByteArray(Charsets.US_ASCII))
                    output.flush()
                }
            } catch (error: Throwable) {
                serverFailure.set(error)
            }
        }

        try {
            val tunnel = ProxyTcpTunnel.open(
                profile = ProxyProfile(
                    "SOCKS test", "127.0.0.1", server.localPort, ProxyProtocol.SOCKS5,
                    username = "user", password = "pass", remoteDns = true
                ),
                destinationHost = "example.com",
                destinationPort = 443
            )
            tunnel.use { socket ->
                socket.getOutputStream().write("ping".toByteArray(Charsets.US_ASCII))
                val response = ByteArray(4)
                socket.getInputStream().readFully(response)
                assertEquals("pong", String(response, Charsets.US_ASCII))
            }
            worker.join(3000)
            assertTrue("SOCKS5 CONNECT must use remote hostname and port", requestValid.get())
            serverFailure.get()?.let { throw AssertionError("Mock SOCKS5 proxy failed", it) }
        } finally {
            server.close()
            worker.join(1000)
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun closesSocketWhenProtectionIsRejected() {
        val server = ServerSocket(0)
        server.soTimeout = 250
        try {
            try {
                ProxyTcpTunnel.open(
                    ProxyProfile("HTTP test", "127.0.0.1", server.localPort, ProxyProtocol.HTTP_CONNECT),
                    "example.com",
                    443,
                    protectSocket = { false }
                )
                throw AssertionError("Expected protection failure")
            } catch (expected: java.io.IOException) {
                assertTrue(expected.message.orEmpty().contains("protect"))
            }
            try {
                server.accept().close()
                throw AssertionError("Proxy socket must not connect if protect() fails")
            } catch (_: java.net.SocketTimeoutException) {
                // Expected: connection was rejected before connect().
            }
        } finally {
            server.close()
        }
    }

    private fun readHttpHeaders(input: BufferedInputStream): String {
        val result = StringBuilder()
        var matched = 0
        val ending = "\r\n\r\n"
        while (matched < ending.length) {
            val value = input.read()
            if (value < 0) throw AssertionError("Incomplete HTTP CONNECT request")
            val char = value.toChar()
            result.append(char)
            matched = if (char == ending[matched]) matched + 1
            else if (char == '\r') 1 else 0
        }
        return result.toString()
    }

    private fun readExactly(input: BufferedInputStream, count: Int): ByteArray {
        val bytes = ByteArray(count)
        input.readFully(bytes)
        return bytes
    }

    private fun java.io.InputStream.readFully(bytes: ByteArray) {
        var offset = 0
        while (offset < bytes.size) {
            val count = read(bytes, offset, bytes.size - offset)
            if (count < 0) throw AssertionError("Unexpected end of mock proxy stream")
            offset += count
        }
    }

}

package com.aboezzat.vpn.proxy

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Test

class ProxyTestRequestParserTest {
    private fun parse(
        name: String? = "Phone proxy",
        host: String? = "192.168.1.10",
        port: Int = 8080,
        protocol: String? = ProxyProtocol.HTTP_CONNECT.name,
        username: String? = null,
        password: String? = null,
        remoteDns: Boolean = true
    ) = ProxyTestRequestParser.parse(name, host, port, protocol, username, password, remoteDns)

    @Test fun parsesValidHttpProfile() {
        val profile = parse()
        assertNotNull(profile)
        assertEquals(ProxyProtocol.HTTP_CONNECT, profile?.protocol)
    }

    @Test fun defaultsMissingProtocolToHttpConnect() {
        assertEquals(ProxyProtocol.HTTP_CONNECT, parse(protocol = null)?.protocol)
    }

    @Test fun parsesSocks5AndPreservesOptions() {
        val profile = parse(
            protocol = ProxyProtocol.SOCKS5.name,
            username = "user",
            password = "secret",
            remoteDns = false
        )
        assertEquals(ProxyProtocol.SOCKS5, profile?.protocol)
        assertEquals("user", profile?.username)
        assertEquals("secret", profile?.password)
        assertEquals(false, profile?.remoteDns)
    }

    @Test fun rejectsUnknownProtocol() {
        assertNull(parse(protocol = "SOCKS4"))
    }

    @Test fun rejectsMissingNameOrHost() {
        assertNull(parse(name = null))
        assertNull(parse(host = null))
    }

    @Test fun rejectsInvalidPort() {
        assertNull(parse(port = 0))
        assertNull(parse(port = 65536))
    }

    @Test fun rejectsIncompleteCredentials() {
        assertNull(parse(username = "user"))
        assertNull(parse(password = "secret"))
    }
}

package com.aboezzat.vpn.proxy

import java.io.BufferedInputStream
import java.io.BufferedOutputStream
import java.net.DatagramPacket
import java.net.DatagramSocket
import java.net.InetAddress
import java.net.ServerSocket
import java.net.Socket
import kotlin.concurrent.thread
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class Socks5UdpAssociateTesterTest {
    @Test
    fun verifiesUdpDatagramRoundTripThroughRelay() {
        DatagramSocket(0, InetAddress.getByName("127.0.0.1")).use { relay ->
            relay.soTimeout = 3000
            ServerSocket(0).use { server ->
                val worker = thread(start = true) {
                    server.accept().use { socket ->
                        val input = BufferedInputStream(socket.getInputStream())
                        val output = BufferedOutputStream(socket.getOutputStream())
                        assertEquals(listOf(5, 1, 0), readExactly(input, 3).map { it.toInt() and 0xff })
                        output.write(byteArrayOf(5, 0))
                        output.flush()

                        assertEquals(
                            listOf(5, 3, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0),
                            readExactly(input, 10).map { it.toInt() and 0xff }
                        )
                        val address = byteArrayOf(127, 0, 0, 1)
                        val port = relay.localPort
                        output.write(byteArrayOf(5, 0, 0, 1) + address +
                            byteArrayOf((port ushr 8).toByte(), port.toByte()))
                        output.flush()

                        val requestBytes = ByteArray(2048)
                        val request = DatagramPacket(requestBytes, requestBytes.size)
                        relay.receive(request)
                        val packet = request.data.copyOf(request.length)
                        // SOCKS UDP header is 10 bytes for an IPv4 destination.
                        assertTrue(packet.size > 22)
                        val dnsQuery = packet.copyOfRange(10, packet.size)
                        dnsQuery[2] = (dnsQuery[2].toInt() or 0x80).toByte()
                        val response = byteArrayOf(0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 0, 53) + dnsQuery
                        relay.send(DatagramPacket(response, response.size, request.address, request.port))
                    }
                }

                val result = Socks5UdpAssociateTester.test(
                    ProxyProfile("UDP test", "127.0.0.1", server.localPort, ProxyProtocol.SOCKS5)
                )
                worker.join(4000)
                assertTrue(result.message, result.supported)
                assertEquals("127.0.0.1", result.relayAddress)
                assertEquals(relay.localPort, result.relayPort)
                assertTrue(result.message.contains("round-trip verified"))
            }
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun clearlyReportsHttpConnectCannotCarryUdp() {
        val result = Socks5UdpAssociateTester.test(
            ProxyProfile("HTTP", "127.0.0.1", 8080, ProxyProtocol.HTTP_CONNECT)
        )
        assertFalse(result.supported)
        assertTrue(result.message.contains("TCP only"))
    }

    private fun readExactly(input: BufferedInputStream, count: Int): ByteArray {
        val result = ByteArray(count)
        for (index in 0 until count) {
            val value = input.read()
            if (value < 0) throw AssertionError("Unexpected end of SOCKS5 request")
            result[index] = value.toByte()
        }
        return result
    }
}

package com.aboezzat.vpn.proxy

import java.net.ServerSocket
import java.net.Socket
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch
import java.util.concurrent.Executors
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit
import kotlin.concurrent.thread
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class TcpStreamRelayTest {
    @Test
    fun relaysBytesInBothDirectionsAndClosesAfterBothHalfCloses() {
        val clientListener = ServerSocket(0)
        val upstreamListener = ServerSocket(0)
        val pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2)
        val closed = CountDownLatch(1)
        val clientPeer = Socket("127.0.0.1", clientListener.localPort)
        val clientRelay = clientListener.accept()
        val upstreamPeer = Socket("127.0.0.1", upstreamListener.localPort)
        val upstreamRelay = upstreamListener.accept()
        val session = TcpStreamRelay.start(clientRelay, upstreamRelay, pool) { closed.countDown() }

        try {
            clientPeer.getOutputStream().write("client-to-upstream".toByteArray())
            clientPeer.shutdownOutput()
            val upstreamReceived = upstreamPeer.getInputStream().readNBytes(19)
            assertEquals("client-to-upstream", String(upstreamReceived))

            upstreamPeer.getOutputStream().write("upstream-to-client".toByteArray())
            upstreamPeer.shutdownOutput()
            val clientReceived = clientPeer.getInputStream().readNBytes(19)
            assertEquals("upstream-to-client", String(clientReceived))

            assertTrue(closed.await(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
            assertTrue(session.isClosed)
        } finally {
            session.close()
            clientPeer.close()
            upstreamPeer.close()
            clientListener.close()
            upstreamListener.close()
            pool.shutdownNow()
            pool.awaitTermination(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun manualCloseClosesBothSockets() {
        val listenerA = ServerSocket(0)
        val listenerB = ServerSocket(0)
        val pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2)
        val aPeer = Socket("127.0.0.1", listenerA.localPort)
        val aRelay = listenerA.accept()
        val bPeer = Socket("127.0.0.1", listenerB.localPort)
        val bRelay = listenerB.accept()
        val session = TcpStreamRelay.start(aRelay, bRelay, pool)
        try {
            session.close()
            assertTrue(session.isClosed)
            assertTrue(aRelay.isClosed)
            assertTrue(bRelay.isClosed)
        } finally {
            aPeer.close()
            bPeer.close()
            listenerA.close()
            listenerB.close()
            pool.shutdownNow()
        }
    }
}

package com.aboezzat.vpn.vpn

import com.aboezzat.vpn.core.AppRoutePolicyResolver
import com.aboezzat.vpn.core.AppRouteResolutionReason
import com.aboezzat.vpn.core.RouteMode
import com.aboezzat.vpn.core.UidPackageLookup
import java.net.InetAddress
import java.net.InetSocketAddress
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class ConnectionRouteDecisionEngineTest {
    private val tuple = NetworkConnectionTuple(
        IpTransportProtocol.TCP,
        InetSocketAddress(InetAddress.getByName("10.0.0.2"), 43120),
        InetSocketAddress(InetAddress.getByName("203.0.113.10"), 443)
    )

    @Test
    fun resolvesUidThenAppliesExplicitPackageRoute() {
        val engine = ConnectionRouteDecisionEngine(
            ConnectionOwnerUidProvider { ConnectionOwnerUidResult.Found(10123) },
            AppRoutePolicyResolver(UidPackageLookup { listOf("com.example.app") })
        )

        val decision = engine.decide(
            tuple,
            mapOf("com.example.app" to RouteMode.PROXY_ONLY)
        )

        assertTrue(decision is ConnectionRouteDecision.Resolved)
        decision as ConnectionRouteDecision.Resolved
        assertEquals(10123, decision.ownerUid)
        assertEquals(RouteMode.PROXY_ONLY, decision.policy.mode)
        assertEquals(AppRouteResolutionReason.EXPLICIT_POLICY, decision.policy.reason)
    }

    @Test
    fun missingConnectionOwnerIsNotGuessedOrRouted() {
        val engine = ConnectionRouteDecisionEngine(
            ConnectionOwnerUidProvider { ConnectionOwnerUidResult.ConnectionNotFound },
            AppRoutePolicyResolver(UidPackageLookup { listOf("com.example.app") })
        )

        val decision = engine.decide(
            tuple,
            mapOf("com.example.app" to RouteMode.DIRECT)
        )

        assertTrue(decision is ConnectionRouteDecision.Unresolved)
        decision as ConnectionRouteDecision.Unresolved
        assertEquals(ConnectionOwnerUidResult.ConnectionNotFound, decision.ownerLookup)
    }

    @Test
    fun unsupportedAndroidVersionRemainsExplicitlyUnsupported() {
        val engine = ConnectionRouteDecisionEngine(
            ConnectionOwnerUidProvider { ConnectionOwnerUidResult.UnsupportedAndroidVersion },
            AppRoutePolicyResolver(UidPackageLookup { emptyList() })
        )

        val decision = engine.decide(tuple, emptyMap())

        assertTrue(decision is ConnectionRouteDecision.Unresolved)
        decision as ConnectionRouteDecision.Unresolved
        assertEquals(
            ConnectionOwnerUidResult.UnsupportedAndroidVersion,
            decision.ownerLookup
        )
    }

    @Test
    fun invalidUidReturnedByProviderIsRejected() {
        val engine = ConnectionRouteDecisionEngine(
            ConnectionOwnerUidProvider { ConnectionOwnerUidResult.Found(-1) },
            AppRoutePolicyResolver(UidPackageLookup { listOf("com.example.app") })
        )

        val decision = engine.decide(tuple, emptyMap())

        assertTrue(decision is ConnectionRouteDecision.Unresolved)
        decision as ConnectionRouteDecision.Unresolved
        assertEquals(ConnectionOwnerUidResult.ConnectionNotFound, decision.ownerLookup)
    }
}

package com.aboezzat.vpn.vpn

import com.aboezzat.vpn.proxy.ProxyProfile
import com.aboezzat.vpn.proxy.ProxyProtocol
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertThrows
import org.junit.Test

class DnsOverTlsForwarderTest {
    @Test
    fun rejectsMalformedQueryBeforeOpeningProxySocket() {
        val forwarder = DnsOverTlsForwarder()
        val profile = ProxyProfile(
            name = "test",
            host = "127.0.0.1",
            port = 1,
            protocol = ProxyProtocol.HTTP_CONNECT
        )
        val error = assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            forwarder.forward(profile, "resolver.example", ByteArray(12))
        }
        assertEquals(
            "DNS query is malformed or uses an unsupported query layout.",
            error.message
        )
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsBlankResolverBeforeOpeningProxySocket() {
        val forwarder = DnsOverTlsForwarder()
        val profile = ProxyProfile(
            name = "test",
            host = "127.0.0.1",
            port = 1,
            protocol = ProxyProtocol.HTTP_CONNECT
        )
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            forwarder.forward(profile, " ", validQuery())
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsInvalidTimeoutAndMessageLimit() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            DnsOverTlsForwarder(connectTimeoutMs = 0)
        }
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            DnsOverTlsForwarder(maxMessageBytes = 11)
        }
    }

    private fun validQuery(): ByteArray = byteArrayOf(
        0x12, 0x34, 0x01, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01,
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
        0x07, 'e'.code.toByte(), 'x'.code.toByte(), 'a'.code.toByte(),
        'm'.code.toByte(), 'p'.code.toByte(), 'l'.code.toByte(), 'e'.code.toByte(),
        0x03, 'c'.code.toByte(), 'o'.code.toByte(), 'm'.code.toByte(),
        0x00, 0x00, 0x01, 0x00, 0x01
    )
}

المجلد: app/src/test/java/com/aboezzat/vpn/vpn

package com.aboezzat.vpn.vpn

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class DnsQueryParserTest {
    @Test
    fun parsesSingleAQuestion() {
        val parsed = DnsQueryParser.parse(query("Example.COM", type = 1, rd = true))
        assertEquals(0x1234, parsed?.transactionId)
        assertEquals("example.com", parsed?.name)
        assertEquals(1, parsed?.type)
        assertEquals(1, parsed?.queryClass)
        assertTrue(parsed?.recursionDesired == true)
        assertFalse(parsed?.ednsPresent ?: true)
    }

    @Test
    fun parsesAaaaQuestionAndRecursionFlag() {
        val parsed = DnsQueryParser.parse(query("ipv6.example", type = 28, rd = false))
        assertEquals(28, parsed?.type)
        assertFalse(parsed?.recursionDesired ?: true)
    }

    @Test
    fun parsesEdnsOptAndDnssecOk() {
        val parsed = DnsQueryParser.parse(query("secure.example", 1, true, includeEdns = true))
        assertTrue(parsed?.ednsPresent == true)
        assertEquals(1232, parsed?.udpPayloadSize)
        assertEquals(0, parsed?.ednsVersion)
        assertTrue(parsed?.dnssecOk == true)
        assertEquals(0, parsed?.ednsExtendedRcode)
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsMalformedEdnsAndUnsupportedQueries() {
        val valid = query("example.com", 1, true)
        val response = valid.copyOf().also { it[2] = 0x81.toByte() }
        assertNull(DnsQueryParser.parse(response))
        assertNull(DnsQueryParser.parse(valid.copyOf(valid.size - 2)))
        assertNull(DnsQueryParser.parse(ByteArray(10)))
        val multipleQuestions = valid.copyOf().also { it[5] = 2 }
        assertNull(DnsQueryParser.parse(multipleQuestions))
        val compressedName = valid.copyOf().also { it[12] = 0xc0.toByte() }
        assertNull(DnsQueryParser.parse(compressedName))

        val badOpt = query("example.com", 1, true, includeEdns = true)
            .also { it[it.size - 1] = 1 } // option data has no option header
        assertNull(DnsQueryParser.parse(badOpt))
    }

    private fun query(
        name: String,
        type: Int,
        rd: Boolean,
        includeEdns: Boolean = false
    ): ByteArray {
        val bytes = ArrayList<Byte>()
        fun add(value: Int) { bytes += value.toByte() }
        add(0x12); add(0x34)
        add(if (rd) 0x01 else 0x00); add(0x00)
        add(0x00); add(0x01) // QDCOUNT
        repeat(4) { add(0) } // ANCOUNT and NSCOUNT
        add(0); add(if (includeEdns) 1 else 0) // ARCOUNT
        name.split(".").forEach { label ->
            add(label.length)
            label.toByteArray(Charsets.US_ASCII).forEach { bytes += it }
        }
        add(0)
        add(type shr 8); add(type and 0xff)
        add(0); add(1) // IN

        if (includeEdns) {
            add(0) // root owner name
            add(0); add(41) // OPT
            add(0x04); add(0xd0) // UDP payload size 1232
            add(0); add(0) // extended RCODE and EDNS version
            add(0x80); add(0) // DO bit
            add(0); add(0) // RDLEN
        }
        return bytes.toByteArray()
    }
}

package com.aboezzat.vpn.vpn

import com.aboezzat.vpn.proxy.ProxyProfile
import com.aboezzat.vpn.proxy.ProxyProtocol
import org.junit.Assert.assertArrayEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertThrows
import org.junit.Test
import java.io.BufferedInputStream
import java.io.BufferedOutputStream
import java.io.DataInputStream
import java.io.DataOutputStream
import java.net.ServerSocket
import java.net.Socket
import java.util.concurrent.Executors
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

class DnsTcpForwarderTest {
    @Test
    fun forwardsLengthPrefixedDnsMessageThroughHttpConnectProxy() {
        ServerSocket(0).use { proxyServer ->
            val executor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()
            try {
                val proxyTask = executor.submit {
                    proxyServer.accept().use { socket ->
                        val input = BufferedInputStream(socket.getInputStream())
                        val output = BufferedOutputStream(socket.getOutputStream())
                        val request = readHeaders(input)
                        check(request.startsWith("CONNECT resolver.test:53 "))
                        output.write("HTTP/1.1 200 Connection Established\r\n\r\n".toByteArray())
                        output.flush()

                        val dataIn = DataInputStream(input)
                        val queryLength = dataIn.readUnsignedShort()
                        val query = ByteArray(queryLength)
                        dataIn.readFully(query)
                        val response = query.copyOf().also {
                            it[2] = 0x81.toByte()
                            it[3] = 0x80.toByte()
                        }
                        val dataOut = DataOutputStream(output)
                        dataOut.writeShort(response.size)
                        dataOut.write(response)
                        dataOut.flush()
                    }
                }

                val query = dnsQuery()
                val result = DnsTcpForwarder().forward(
                    profile = ProxyProfile(
                        name = "local test proxy",
                        host = "127.0.0.1",
                        port = proxyServer.localPort,
                        protocol = ProxyProtocol.HTTP_CONNECT
                    ),
                    resolverHost = "resolver.test",
                    query = query
                )
                assertArrayEquals(query.copyOf().also {
                    it[2] = 0x81.toByte()
                    it[3] = 0x80.toByte()
                }, result)
                proxyTask.get(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
            } finally {
                executor.shutdownNow()
            }
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsMalformedDnsQueryBeforeOpeningProxySocket() {
        val forwarder = DnsTcpForwarder()
        val profile = ProxyProfile(
            name = "test",
            host = "127.0.0.1",
            port = 1,
            protocol = ProxyProtocol.HTTP_CONNECT
        )
        val error = assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            forwarder.forward(profile, "127.0.0.1", ByteArray(12))
        }
        assertEquals("DNS query is malformed or uses an unsupported query layout.", error.message)
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsInvalidConstructorLimits() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            DnsTcpForwarder(maxMessageBytes = 11)
        }
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            DnsTcpForwarder(readTimeoutMs = 0)
        }
    }

    private fun readHeaders(input: BufferedInputStream): String {
        val bytes = ArrayList<Byte>()
        var matched = 0
        while (matched < 4) {
            val value = input.read()
            check(value >= 0)
            bytes += value.toByte()
            matched = when {
                matched == 0 && value == 13 -> 1
                matched == 1 && value == 10 -> 2
                matched == 2 && value == 13 -> 3
                matched == 3 && value == 10 -> 4
                value == 13 -> 1
                else -> 0
            }
        }
        return bytes.toByteArray().toString(Charsets.US_ASCII)
    }

    private fun dnsQuery(): ByteArray = byteArrayOf(
        0x12, 0x34, 0x01, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01,
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
        0x07, 'e'.code.toByte(), 'x'.code.toByte(), 'a'.code.toByte(),
        'm'.code.toByte(), 'p'.code.toByte(), 'l'.code.toByte(), 'e'.code.toByte(),
        0x03, 'c'.code.toByte(), 'o'.code.toByte(), 'm'.code.toByte(),
        0x00, 0x00, 0x01, 0x00, 0x01
    )
}

package com.aboezzat.vpn.vpn

import com.aboezzat.vpn.proxy.ProxyProfile
import com.aboezzat.vpn.proxy.ProxyProtocol
import org.junit.Assert.assertArrayEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import java.io.BufferedInputStream
import java.io.BufferedOutputStream
import java.io.DataInputStream
import java.io.DataOutputStream
import java.net.DatagramPacket
import java.net.DatagramSocket
import java.net.InetAddress
import java.net.ServerSocket
import java.util.concurrent.Executors
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

class DnsUdpTcpBridgeTest {
    @Test
    fun receivesLocalUdpQueryAndReturnsDnsOverTcpProxyResponse() {
        ServerSocket(0).use { proxyServer ->
            val executor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()
            val proxyTask = executor.submit {
                proxyServer.accept().use { socket ->
                    val input = BufferedInputStream(socket.getInputStream())
                    val output = BufferedOutputStream(socket.getOutputStream())
                    val headers = readHeaders(input)
                    check(headers.startsWith("CONNECT resolver.test:53 "))
                    output.write("HTTP/1.1 200 Connection Established\r\n\r\n".toByteArray())
                    output.flush()

                    val dataIn = DataInputStream(input)
                    val queryLength = dataIn.readUnsignedShort()
                    val query = ByteArray(queryLength)
                    dataIn.readFully(query)
                    val response = dnsResponse(query)
                    val dataOut = DataOutputStream(output)
                    dataOut.writeShort(response.size)
                    dataOut.write(response)
                    dataOut.flush()
                }
            }

            val profile = ProxyProfile(
                name = "bridge test",
                host = "127.0.0.1",
                port = proxyServer.localPort,
                protocol = ProxyProtocol.HTTP_CONNECT
            )
            val bridge = DnsUdpTcpBridge()
            bridge.use {
                val local = bridge.start(profile, "resolver.test")
                assertEqualsLoopback(local.address.hostAddress)
                DatagramSocket().use { client ->
                    client.soTimeout = 4_000
                    val query = dnsQuery()
                    client.send(DatagramPacket(query, query.size, local))
                    val buffer = ByteArray(2_048)
                    val packet = DatagramPacket(buffer, buffer.size)
                    client.receive(packet)
                    assertArrayEquals(dnsResponse(query), packet.data.copyOf(packet.length))
                }
                proxyTask.get(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
                assertTrue(bridge.stats().received >= 1)
                val forwardingDeadline = System.nanoTime() + TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(2)
                while (bridge.stats().forwarded < 1 && System.nanoTime() < forwardingDeadline) {
                    Thread.sleep(10)
                }
                assertTrue(bridge.stats().forwarded >= 1)
                assertTrue(bridge.isRunning)
            }
            assertFalse(bridge.isRunning)
            val restarted = bridge.start(profile, "resolver.test")
            assertEqualsLoopback(restarted.address.hostAddress)
            assertTrue(bridge.isRunning)
            bridge.close()
            assertFalse(bridge.isRunning)
            executor.shutdownNow()
        }
    }

    private fun assertEqualsLoopback(value: String) {
        org.junit.Assert.assertEquals("127.0.0.1", value)
    }

    private fun readHeaders(input: BufferedInputStream): String {
        val bytes = ArrayList<Byte>()
        var matched = 0
        while (matched < 4) {
            val value = input.read()
            check(value >= 0)
            bytes += value.toByte()
            matched = when {
                matched == 0 && value == 13 -> 1
                matched == 1 && value == 10 -> 2
                matched == 2 && value == 13 -> 3
                matched == 3 && value == 10 -> 4
                value == 13 -> 1
                else -> 0
            }
        }
        return bytes.toByteArray().toString(Charsets.US_ASCII)
    }

    private fun dnsQuery(): ByteArray = byteArrayOf(
        0x12, 0x34, 0x01, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01,
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
        0x07, 'e'.code.toByte(), 'x'.code.toByte(), 'a'.code.toByte(),
        'm'.code.toByte(), 'p'.code.toByte(), 'l'.code.toByte(), 'e'.code.toByte(),
        0x03, 'c'.code.toByte(), 'o'.code.toByte(), 'm'.code.toByte(),
        0x00, 0x00, 0x01, 0x00, 0x01
    )

    private fun dnsResponse(query: ByteArray): ByteArray =
        query.copyOf().also {
            it[2] = 0x81.toByte()
            it[3] = 0x80.toByte()
        }
}

package com.aboezzat.vpn.vpn

import java.net.InetAddress
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertThrows
import org.junit.Test

class Ipv4ConnectionTupleParserTest {
    @Test
    fun parsesOutboundTcpTuple() {
        val tuple = Ipv4ConnectionTupleParser.parseOutbound(packet(protocol = 6, sourcePort = 43120, destinationPort = 443))
        assertEquals(IpTransportProtocol.TCP, tuple.protocol)
        assertEquals("10.0.0.2", tuple.local.address.hostAddress)
        assertEquals(43120, tuple.local.port)
        assertEquals("203.0.113.10", tuple.remote.address.hostAddress)
        assertEquals(443, tuple.remote.port)
    }

    @Test
    fun parsesOutboundUdpTuple() {
        val tuple = Ipv4ConnectionTupleParser.parseOutbound(packet(protocol = 17, sourcePort = 53000, destinationPort = 53))
        assertEquals(IpTransportProtocol.UDP, tuple.protocol)
        assertEquals(53000, tuple.local.port)
        assertEquals(53, tuple.remote.port)
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsIpv6AndUnknownProtocols() {
        val ipv6 = packet(17).also { it[0] = 0x60 }
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            Ipv4ConnectionTupleParser.parseOutbound(ipv6)
        }
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            Ipv4ConnectionTupleParser.parseOutbound(packet(1))
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsBadIpChecksum() {
        val bytes = packet(17)
        bytes[8] = 63
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            Ipv4ConnectionTupleParser.parseOutbound(bytes)
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsFragmentedPackets() {
        val bytes = packet(17)
        put16(bytes, 6, 0x2000)
        fixIpChecksum(bytes)
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            Ipv4ConnectionTupleParser.parseOutbound(bytes)
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsInvalidTcpDataOffset() {
        val bytes = packet(6)
        bytes[32] = 0x40
        fixIpChecksum(bytes)
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            Ipv4ConnectionTupleParser.parseOutbound(bytes)
        }
    }

    private fun packet(
        protocol: Int,
        sourcePort: Int = 43120,
        destinationPort: Int = 443
    ): ByteArray {
        val transportLength = if (protocol == 6) 20 else 8
        val bytes = ByteArray(20 + transportLength)
        bytes[0] = 0x45
        put16(bytes, 2, bytes.size)
        put16(bytes, 4, 0x1234)
        bytes[8] = 64
        bytes[9] = protocol.toByte()
        byteArrayOf(10, 0, 0, 2).copyInto(bytes, 12)
        byteArrayOf(203.toByte(), 0, 113, 10).copyInto(bytes, 16)
        put16(bytes, 20, sourcePort)
        put16(bytes, 22, destinationPort)
        if (protocol == 6) {
            bytes[32] = 0x50
        } else {
            put16(bytes, 24, 8)
        }
        fixIpChecksum(bytes)
        return bytes
    }

    private fun fixIpChecksum(bytes: ByteArray) {
        bytes[10] = 0
        bytes[11] = 0
        var sum = 0
        for (i in 0 until 20 step 2) {
            sum += ((bytes[i].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or (bytes[i + 1].toInt() and 0xff)
        }
        while ((sum ushr 16) != 0) sum = (sum and 0xffff) + (sum ushr 16)
        val checksum = sum.inv() and 0xffff
        put16(bytes, 10, checksum)
    }

    private fun put16(bytes: ByteArray, offset: Int, value: Int) {
        bytes[offset] = (value ushr 8).toByte()
        bytes[offset + 1] = value.toByte()
    }
}

اسم الملف: app/src/test/java/com/aboezzat/vpn/vpn/Ipv4TcpPacketParserTest.kt
المجلد: app/src/test/java/com/aboezzat/vpn/vpn

package com.aboezzat.vpn.vpn

import org.junit.Assert.assertArrayEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Assert.assertThrows
import org.junit.Test

class Ipv4TcpPacketParserTest {
    @Test
    fun parsesTcpHeaderFlagsSequenceAndPayload() {
        val bytes = tcpPacket(payload = byteArrayOf(1, 2, 3, 4))
        val parsed = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.parse(bytes)

        assertEquals(IpTransportProtocol.TCP, parsed.tuple.protocol)
        assertEquals(43120, parsed.tuple.local.port)
        assertEquals(443, parsed.tuple.remote.port)
        assertEquals(0xf1234567L, parsed.sequenceNumber)
        assertEquals(0x89abcdefL, parsed.acknowledgementNumber)
        assertEquals(0x18, parsed.flags)
        assertTrue(parsed.isPsh)
        assertTrue(parsed.isAck)
        assertFalse(parsed.isSyn)
        assertEquals(32768, parsed.windowSize)
        assertArrayEquals(byteArrayOf(1, 2, 3, 4), parsed.payload)
        assertEquals(20, parsed.tcpHeaderLength)
        assertTrue(parsed.tcpOptions.isEmpty())
    }

    @Test
    fun parsesTcpOptionsAndNsFlag() {
        val bytes = tcpPacket(
            options = byteArrayOf(1, 1, 0, 0),
            flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_SYN or Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_NS
        )
        val parsed = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.parse(bytes)
        assertEquals(24, parsed.tcpHeaderLength)
        assertTrue(parsed.isSyn)
        assertTrue(parsed.isNs)
        assertArrayEquals(byteArrayOf(1, 1, 0, 0), parsed.tcpOptions)
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsInvalidTcpChecksum() {
        val bytes = tcpPacket(payload = byteArrayOf(9))
        bytes[bytes.lastIndex] = 8
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            Ipv4TcpPacketParser.parse(bytes)
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsMalformedTcpOptionLength() {
        val bytes = tcpPacket(options = byteArrayOf(2, 1, 0, 0))
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            Ipv4TcpPacketParser.parse(bytes)
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsUdpPackets() {
        val bytes = tcpPacket()
        bytes[9] = 17
        fixIpChecksum(bytes)
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            Ipv4TcpPacketParser.parse(bytes)
        }
    }

    private fun tcpPacket(
        payload: ByteArray = byteArrayOf(),
        options: ByteArray = byteArrayOf(),
        flags: Int = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_PSH or Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_ACK
    ): ByteArray {
        require(options.size % 4 == 0)
        val ipLength = 20
        val tcpHeaderLength = 20 + options.size
        val tcpLength = tcpHeaderLength + payload.size
        val bytes = ByteArray(ipLength + tcpLength)
        bytes[0] = 0x45
        put16(bytes, 2, bytes.size)
        put16(bytes, 4, 0x1234)
        bytes[8] = 64
        bytes[9] = 6
        byteArrayOf(10, 0, 0, 2).copyInto(bytes, 12)
        byteArrayOf(203.toByte(), 0, 113, 10).copyInto(bytes, 16)

        val tcp = ipLength
        put16(bytes, tcp, 43120)
        put16(bytes, tcp + 2, 443)
        put32(bytes, tcp + 4, 0xf1234567L)
        put32(bytes, tcp + 8, 0x89abcdefL)
        bytes[tcp + 12] = ((tcpHeaderLength / 4) shl 4 or ((flags ushr 8) and 1)).toByte()
        bytes[tcp + 13] = flags.toByte()
        put16(bytes, tcp + 14, 32768)
        put16(bytes, tcp + 18, 0)
        options.copyInto(bytes, tcp + 20)
        payload.copyInto(bytes, tcp + tcpHeaderLength)
        put16(bytes, tcp + 16, tcpChecksum(bytes, tcp, tcpLength))
        fixIpChecksum(bytes)
        return bytes
    }

    private fun tcpChecksum(bytes: ByteArray, offset: Int, length: Int): Int {
        var sum = 0L
        for (i in 12 until 20 step 2) {
            sum += ((bytes[i].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or (bytes[i + 1].toInt() and 0xff)
        }
        sum += 6
        sum += length
        var index = 0
        while (index + 1 < length) {
            sum += ((bytes[offset + index].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or
                (bytes[offset + index + 1].toInt() and 0xff)
            index += 2
        }
        if (index < length) sum += (bytes[offset + index].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8
        while ((sum ushr 16) != 0L) sum = (sum and 0xffff) + (sum ushr 16)
        return sum.toInt().inv() and 0xffff
    }

    private fun fixIpChecksum(bytes: ByteArray) {
        bytes[10] = 0
        bytes[11] = 0
        var sum = 0
        for (i in 0 until 20 step 2) {
            sum += ((bytes[i].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or (bytes[i + 1].toInt() and 0xff)
        }
        while ((sum ushr 16) != 0) sum = (sum and 0xffff) + (sum ushr 16)
        put16(bytes, 10, sum.inv() and 0xffff)
    }

    private fun put16(bytes: ByteArray, offset: Int, value: Int) {
        bytes[offset] = (value ushr 8).toByte()
        bytes[offset + 1] = value.toByte()
    }

    private fun put32(bytes: ByteArray, offset: Int, value: Long) {
        put16(bytes, offset, (value ushr 16).toInt() and 0xffff)
        put16(bytes, offset + 2, value.toInt() and 0xffff)
    }
}

package com.aboezzat.vpn.vpn

import org.junit.Assert.assertArrayEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertThrows
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class Ipv4UdpDnsPacketCodecTest {
    @Test
    fun parsesDnsUdpQueryAndBuildsReverseResponseWithValidChecksums() {
        val dns = dnsQuery()
        val packet = makeIpv4UdpPacket(
            source = byteArrayOf(192.toByte(), 0, 2, 10),
            destination = byteArrayOf(192.toByte(), 0, 2, 53),
            sourcePort = 53000,
            destinationPort = 53,
            dns = dns
        )
        val parsed = Ipv4UdpDnsPacketCodec.parseQuery(packet)
        assertEquals(53000, parsed.sourcePort)
        assertEquals(53, parsed.destinationPort)
        assertArrayEquals(dns, parsed.dnsPayload)

        val responseDns = dns.copyOf().also {
            it[2] = 0x81.toByte()
            it[3] = 0x80.toByte()
        }
        val response = Ipv4UdpDnsPacketCodec.buildResponse(parsed, responseDns)
        val parsedResponse = Ipv4UdpDnsPacketCodec.parseResponseForTest(response)
        assertEquals(53, parsedResponse.first)
        assertEquals(53000, parsedResponse.second)
        assertArrayEquals(responseDns, parsedResponse.third)
        assertTrue("Generated UDP checksum must validate", udpChecksumValid(response))
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsReservedIpv4Flag() {
        val packet = makeIpv4UdpPacket(
            byteArrayOf(10, 0, 0, 2), byteArrayOf(10, 0, 0, 1),
            50000, 53, dnsQuery()
        )
        putU16(packet, 6, 0x8000)
        putU16(packet, 10, 0)
        putU16(packet, 10, ipv4Checksum(packet, 20))

        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            Ipv4UdpDnsPacketCodec.parseQuery(packet)
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsWrongDestinationPortAndCorruptHeaderChecksum() {
        val dns = dnsQuery()
        val wrongPort = makeIpv4UdpPacket(
            byteArrayOf(10, 0, 0, 2), byteArrayOf(10, 0, 0, 1),
            50000, 5353, dns
        )
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            Ipv4UdpDnsPacketCodec.parseQuery(wrongPort)
        }

        val corrupt = makeIpv4UdpPacket(
            byteArrayOf(10, 0, 0, 2), byteArrayOf(10, 0, 0, 1),
            50000, 53, dns
        )
        corrupt[8] = 10
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            Ipv4UdpDnsPacketCodec.parseQuery(corrupt)
        }
    }

    private fun makeIpv4UdpPacket(
        source: ByteArray,
        destination: ByteArray,
        sourcePort: Int,
        destinationPort: Int,
        dns: ByteArray
    ): ByteArray {
        val packet = ByteArray(20 + 8 + dns.size)
        packet[0] = 0x45
        putU16(packet, 2, packet.size)
        putU16(packet, 4, 0x1234)
        putU16(packet, 6, 0x4000)
        packet[8] = 64
        packet[9] = 17
        source.copyInto(packet, 12)
        destination.copyInto(packet, 16)
        val udp = 20
        putU16(packet, udp, sourcePort)
        putU16(packet, udp + 2, destinationPort)
        putU16(packet, udp + 4, 8 + dns.size)
        dns.copyInto(packet, udp + 8)
        putU16(packet, 10, ipv4Checksum(packet, 20))
        return packet
    }

    private fun udpChecksumValid(packet: ByteArray): Boolean {
        val ihl = (packet[0].toInt() and 0x0f) * 4
        val udpLength = ((packet[ihl + 4].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or
            (packet[ihl + 5].toInt() and 0xff)
        var sum = 0L
        for (offset in listOf(12, 16)) {
            for (i in offset until offset + 4 step 2) {
                sum += ((packet[i].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or
                    (packet[i + 1].toInt() and 0xff)
            }
        }
        sum += 17
        sum += udpLength
        for (i in ihl until ihl + udpLength step 2) {
            val high = (packet[i].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8
            val low = if (i + 1 < ihl + udpLength) packet[i + 1].toInt() and 0xff else 0
            sum += high or low
        }
        while ((sum ushr 16) != 0L) sum = (sum and 0xffff) + (sum ushr 16)
        return (sum.toInt() and 0xffff) == 0xffff
    }

    private fun ipv4Checksum(bytes: ByteArray, length: Int): Int {
        var sum = 0L
        for (i in 0 until length step 2) {
            sum += ((bytes[i].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or
                (bytes[i + 1].toInt() and 0xff)
        }
        while ((sum ushr 16) != 0L) sum = (sum and 0xffff) + (sum ushr 16)
        return sum.toInt().inv() and 0xffff
    }

    private fun putU16(bytes: ByteArray, offset: Int, value: Int) {
        bytes[offset] = (value ushr 8).toByte()
        bytes[offset + 1] = value.toByte()
    }

    private fun dnsQuery(): ByteArray = byteArrayOf(
        0x12, 0x34, 0x01, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01,
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
        0x07, 'e'.code.toByte(), 'x'.code.toByte(), 'a'.code.toByte(),
        'm'.code.toByte(), 'p'.code.toByte(), 'l'.code.toByte(), 'e'.code.toByte(),
        0x03, 'c'.code.toByte(), 'o'.code.toByte(), 'm'.code.toByte(),
        0x00, 0x00, 0x01, 0x00, 0x01
    )
}

/** Test-only minimal decoder to check reply ports and DNS payload. */
private fun Ipv4UdpDnsPacketCodec.parseResponseForTest(packet: ByteArray): Triple<Int, Int, ByteArray> {
    val ihl = (packet[0].toInt() and 0x0f) * 4
    val sourcePort = ((packet[ihl].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or (packet[ihl + 1].toInt() and 0xff)
    val destinationPort = ((packet[ihl + 2].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or (packet[ihl + 3].toInt() and 0xff)
    return Triple(sourcePort, destinationPort, packet.copyOfRange(ihl + 8, packet.size))
}

package com.aboezzat.vpn.vpn

import org.junit.Assert.assertArrayEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Assert.fail
import org.junit.Test

class Ipv4UdpDnsPacketLoopTest {
    @Test
    fun skipsInvalidDnsPacketAndContinuesWithNextPacket() {
        val query = dnsQuery()
        val validPacket = makePacket(query)
        val invalidPacket = validPacket.copyOf().also { it[0] = 0x65 }
        val source = FakeSource(listOf(invalidPacket, validPacket))
        val written = mutableListOf<ByteArray>()
        var resolverCalls = 0
        val processor = Ipv4UdpDnsPacketProcessor(resolver = DnsPacketResolver { dns ->
            resolverCalls++
            dns.copyOf().also {
                it[2] = 0x81.toByte()
                it[3] = 0x80.toByte()
            }
        })
        val loop = Ipv4UdpDnsPacketLoop(
            source = source,
            sink = DnsPacketSink { written += it },
            processor = processor
        )

        loop.run()

        assertEquals(1, resolverCalls)
        assertEquals(1L, loop.processedPackets)
        assertEquals(1L, loop.failedPackets)
        assertEquals(1, written.size)
        assertTrue(source.closed)
        assertEquals(53, u16(written.single(), 20))
        assertEquals(53000, u16(written.single(), 22))
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsSecondRunAttempt() {
        val source = FakeSource(emptyList())
        val loop = Ipv4UdpDnsPacketLoop(
            source = source,
            sink = DnsPacketSink { },
            processor = Ipv4UdpDnsPacketProcessor(resolver = DnsPacketResolver { it })
        )
        loop.run()
        try {
            loop.run()
            fail("Second run must be rejected")
        } catch (_: IllegalStateException) {
            // Expected: a packet source has exactly one owning reader.
        }
        assertTrue(source.closed)
    }

    @Test
    fun stopClosesSourceAndRunExitsAfterCurrentRead() {
        val source = FakeSource(emptyList())
        val loop = Ipv4UdpDnsPacketLoop(
            source = source,
            sink = DnsPacketSink { throw AssertionError("No packet expected") },
            processor = Ipv4UdpDnsPacketProcessor(resolver = DnsPacketResolver { it })
        )

        loop.stop()
        loop.run()

        assertTrue(source.closed)
        assertEquals(0L, loop.processedPackets)
    }

    private class FakeSource(packets: List<ByteArray>) : DnsPacketSource {
        private val queue = ArrayDeque(packets)
        var closed = false
            private set

        override fun readPacket(): ByteArray? = if (closed) null else queue.removeFirstOrNull()
        override fun close() { closed = true }
    }

    private fun makePacket(dns: ByteArray): ByteArray {
        val packet = ByteArray(20 + 8 + dns.size)
        packet[0] = 0x45
        putU16(packet, 2, packet.size)
        putU16(packet, 4, 0x1234)
        putU16(packet, 6, 0x4000)
        packet[8] = 64
        packet[9] = 17
        byteArrayOf(192.toByte(), 0, 2, 10).copyInto(packet, 12)
        byteArrayOf(192.toByte(), 0, 2, 53).copyInto(packet, 16)
        putU16(packet, 20, 53000)
        putU16(packet, 22, 53)
        putU16(packet, 24, 8 + dns.size)
        dns.copyInto(packet, 28)
        putU16(packet, 10, checksum(packet, 20))
        return packet
    }

    private fun checksum(bytes: ByteArray, length: Int): Int {
        var sum = 0L
        for (i in 0 until length step 2) {
            sum += ((bytes[i].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or
                (bytes[i + 1].toInt() and 0xff)
        }
        while ((sum ushr 16) != 0L) sum = (sum and 0xffff) + (sum ushr 16)
        return sum.toInt().inv() and 0xffff
    }

    private fun putU16(bytes: ByteArray, offset: Int, value: Int) {
        bytes[offset] = (value ushr 8).toByte()
        bytes[offset + 1] = value.toByte()
    }

    private fun u16(bytes: ByteArray, offset: Int): Int =
        ((bytes[offset].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or (bytes[offset + 1].toInt() and 0xff)

    private fun dnsQuery(): ByteArray = byteArrayOf(
        0x12, 0x34, 0x01, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01,
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
        0x07, 'e'.code.toByte(), 'x'.code.toByte(), 'a'.code.toByte(),
        'm'.code.toByte(), 'p'.code.toByte(), 'l'.code.toByte(), 'e'.code.toByte(),
        0x03, 'c'.code.toByte(), 'o'.code.toByte(), 'm'.code.toByte(),
        0x00, 0x00, 0x01, 0x00, 0x01
    )
}

package com.aboezzat.vpn.vpn

import org.junit.Assert.assertArrayEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertThrows
import org.junit.Test

class Ipv4UdpDnsPacketProcessorTest {
    @Test
    fun forwardsDnsPayloadAndBuildsReplyPacket() {
        val queryDns = dnsQuery()
        val expectedResponse = queryDns.copyOf().also {
            it[2] = 0x81.toByte()
            it[3] = 0x80.toByte()
        }
        var received: ByteArray? = null
        val processor = Ipv4UdpDnsPacketProcessor(
            resolver = DnsPacketResolver { query ->
                received = query
                expectedResponse
            },
            responseTtl = 42
        )

        val reply = processor.process(packet(queryDns))
        assertArrayEquals(queryDns, received)
        assertEquals(42, reply[8].toInt() and 0xff)
        assertEquals(53, u16(reply, 20))
        assertEquals(53000, u16(reply, 22))
        assertArrayEquals(expectedResponse, reply.copyOfRange(28, reply.size))
        assertEquals(0, checksum(reply, 0, 20))
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsInvalidPacketsBeforeCallingResolver() {
        var called = false
        val processor = Ipv4UdpDnsPacketProcessor(DnsPacketResolver {
            called = true
            it
        })
        val invalid = packet(dnsQuery()).also { it[8] = 0 }
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
            processor.process(invalid)
        }
        assertEquals(false, called)
    }

    private fun packet(dns: ByteArray): ByteArray {
        val result = ByteArray(20 + 8 + dns.size)
        result[0] = 0x45
        putU16(result, 2, result.size)
        putU16(result, 4, 0x2468)
        putU16(result, 6, 0x4000)
        result[8] = 64
        result[9] = 17
        byteArrayOf(10, 0, 0, 2).copyInto(result, 12)
        byteArrayOf(10, 0, 0, 1).copyInto(result, 16)
        putU16(result, 20, 53000)
        putU16(result, 22, 53)
        putU16(result, 24, 8 + dns.size)
        dns.copyInto(result, 28)
        putU16(result, 10, checksum(result, 0, 20))
        return result
    }

    private fun dnsQuery() = byteArrayOf(
        0x12, 0x34, 0x01, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01,
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
        0x07, 0x65, 0x78, 0x61, 0x6d, 0x70, 0x6c, 0x65,
        0x03, 0x63, 0x6f, 0x6d, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01, 0x00, 0x01
    )

    private fun checksum(bytes: ByteArray, offset: Int, length: Int): Int {
        var sum = 0L
        var i = 0
        while (i + 1 < length) {
            sum += ((bytes[offset + i].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or
                (bytes[offset + i + 1].toInt() and 0xff)
            i += 2
        }
        while ((sum ushr 16) != 0L) sum = (sum and 0xffff) + (sum ushr 16)
        return sum.toInt().inv() and 0xffff
    }

    private fun u16(bytes: ByteArray, offset: Int): Int =
        ((bytes[offset].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or (bytes[offset + 1].toInt() and 0xff)

    private fun putU16(bytes: ByteArray, offset: Int, value: Int) {
        bytes[offset] = (value ushr 8).toByte()
        bytes[offset + 1] = value.toByte()
    }
}

package com.aboezzat.vpn.vpn

import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class NativeSocketProtectionTest {

    @Test
    fun rejectsNegativeFileDescriptorWithoutCallingAndroid() {
        var called = false

        val result = NativeSocketProtection.protect(-1) {
            called = true
            true
        }

        assertFalse(result)
        assertFalse(called)
    }

    @Test
    fun returnsAndroidProtectionResult() {
        assertTrue(NativeSocketProtection.protect(42) { it == 42 })
        assertFalse(NativeSocketProtection.protect(42) { false })
    }

    @Test
    fun failsClosedWhenProtectionCallbackThrows() {
        val result = NativeSocketProtection.protect(42) {
            throw IllegalStateException("protection failed")
        }

        assertFalse(result)
    }
}

package com.aboezzat.vpn.vpn

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch
import java.util.concurrent.Executors
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger

class NativeTunnelRunnerTest {
    @Test
    fun keepsTunDescriptorOpenUntilNativeRunReturns() {
        val executor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()
        val nativeStarted = CountDownLatch(1)
        val allowNativeReturn = CountDownLatch(1)
        val finished = CountDownLatch(1)
        val order = mutableListOf<String>()
        try {
            val runner = NativeTunnelRunner(
                executor = executor,
                runEngine = { _, _, _ ->
                    synchronized(order) { order.add("native-started") }
                    nativeStarted.countDown()
                    assertTrue(allowNativeReturn.await(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
                    synchronized(order) { order.add("native-returned") }
                    0
                },
                stopEngine = { 0 },
                onFinished = { finished.countDown() }
            )

            assertTrue(runner.start("args", 1500) {
                synchronized(order) { order.add("tun-closed") }
            })
            assertTrue(nativeStarted.await(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
            assertTrue(runner.isActive())
            allowNativeReturn.countDown()
            assertTrue(finished.await(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS))

            assertEquals(
                listOf("native-started", "native-returned", "tun-closed"),
                synchronized(order) { order.toList() }
            )
            assertFalse(runner.isActive())
        } finally {
            allowNativeReturn.countDown()
            executor.shutdownNow()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsSecondStartAndForwardsStopRequest() {
        val executor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()
        val nativeStarted = CountDownLatch(1)
        val allowNativeReturn = CountDownLatch(1)
        val stopCalls = AtomicInteger()
        val finished = CountDownLatch(1)
        try {
            val runner = NativeTunnelRunner(
                executor = executor,
                runEngine = { _, _, _ ->
                    nativeStarted.countDown()
                    allowNativeReturn.await(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
                    0
                },
                stopEngine = { stopCalls.incrementAndGet(); 0 },
                onFinished = { finished.countDown() }
            )
            assertTrue(runner.start("args", 1500) {})
            assertTrue(nativeStarted.await(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
            assertFalse(runner.start("other", 1500) {})
            assertTrue(runner.stop())
            assertEquals(1, stopCalls.get())
            allowNativeReturn.countDown()
            assertTrue(finished.await(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
        } finally {
            allowNativeReturn.countDown()
            executor.shutdownNow()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun closesDescriptorEvenWhenNativeRunThrows() {
        val executor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()
        val finished = CountDownLatch(1)
        val closed = AtomicInteger()
        val result = AtomicInteger(0)
        try {
            val runner = NativeTunnelRunner(
                executor = executor,
                runEngine = { _, _, _ -> error("native unavailable") },
                stopEngine = { 0 },
                onFinished = { result.set(it); finished.countDown() }
            )
            assertTrue(runner.start("args", 1500) { closed.incrementAndGet() })
            assertTrue(finished.await(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
            assertEquals(1, closed.get())
            assertEquals(NativeTunnelRunner.NATIVE_RUN_EXCEPTION, result.get())
        } finally {
            executor.shutdownNow()
        }
    }
}

package com.aboezzat.vpn.vpn

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class TcpFlowLifecycleTrackerTest {
    @Test
    fun tracksThreeWayHandshakeWithoutClaimingToImplementTcp() {
        val tracker = TcpFlowLifecycleTracker()
        val syn = parse(tcpPacket(flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_SYN))
        val first = tracker.observe(syn, TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.OUTBOUND, 10)
        assertEquals(TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.State.SYN_SENT, (first as TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.ObserveResult.Updated).snapshot.state)

        val synAck = parse(tcpPacket(
            outbound = false,
            flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_SYN or Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_ACK
        ))
        val second = tracker.observe(synAck, TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.INBOUND, 20)
        assertEquals(TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.State.SYN_ACK_RECEIVED, (second as TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.ObserveResult.Updated).snapshot.state)

        val ack = parse(tcpPacket(flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_ACK))
        val third = tracker.observe(ack, TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.OUTBOUND, 30)
        assertEquals(TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.State.ESTABLISHED, (third as TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.ObserveResult.Updated).snapshot.state)
        assertEquals(3L, third.snapshot.packetsSeen)
    }

    @Test
    fun tracksHalfCloseAndBothFinObservations() {
        val tracker = TcpFlowLifecycleTracker()
        tracker.observe(
            parse(tcpPacket(flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_SYN)),
            TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.OUTBOUND, 1
        )
        val localFin = tracker.observe(
            parse(tcpPacket(flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_FIN or Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_ACK)),
            TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.OUTBOUND, 2
        ) as TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.ObserveResult.Updated
        assertEquals(TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.State.LOCAL_HALF_CLOSED, localFin.snapshot.state)

        val remoteFin = tracker.observe(
            parse(tcpPacket(outbound = false, flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_FIN or Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_ACK)),
            TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.INBOUND, 3
        ) as TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.ObserveResult.Updated
        assertEquals(TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.State.CLOSING, remoteFin.snapshot.state)
    }

    @Test
    fun rstClosesFlowAndPruneRemovesAfterRetention() {
        val tracker = TcpFlowLifecycleTracker(closedRetentionMillis = 100)
        tracker.observe(
            parse(tcpPacket(flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_SYN)),
            TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.OUTBOUND, 100
        )
        val closed = tracker.observe(
            parse(tcpPacket(flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_RST)),
            TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.OUTBOUND, 120
        ) as TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.ObserveResult.Updated
        assertEquals(TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.State.CLOSED, closed.snapshot.state)
        assertEquals(0, tracker.prune(219))
        assertEquals(1, tracker.prune(220))
        assertTrue(tracker.snapshot().isEmpty())
    }

    @Test
    fun closingFlowsArePrunedAfterRetention() {
        val tracker = TcpFlowLifecycleTracker(closedRetentionMillis = 50)
        tracker.observe(
            parse(tcpPacket(flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_SYN)),
            TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.OUTBOUND, 100
        )
        tracker.observe(
            parse(tcpPacket(flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_FIN or Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_ACK)),
            TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.OUTBOUND, 110
        )
        val closing = tracker.observe(
            parse(tcpPacket(outbound = false, flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_FIN or Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_ACK)),
            TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.INBOUND, 120
        ) as TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.ObserveResult.Updated
        assertEquals(TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.State.CLOSING, closing.snapshot.state)
        assertEquals(0, tracker.prune(169))
        assertEquals(1, tracker.prune(170))
        assertTrue(tracker.snapshot().isEmpty())
    }

    @Test
    fun newSynReplacesTerminalFlowWhenFourTupleIsReused() {
        val tracker = TcpFlowLifecycleTracker(closedRetentionMillis = 10_000)
        val first = tracker.observe(
            parse(tcpPacket(flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_SYN)),
            TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.OUTBOUND, 10
        ) as TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.ObserveResult.Updated
        tracker.observe(
            parse(tcpPacket(flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_RST)),
            TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.OUTBOUND, 11
        )

        val second = tracker.observe(
            parse(tcpPacket(flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_SYN)),
            TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.OUTBOUND, 12
        ) as TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.ObserveResult.Updated

        assertEquals(first.snapshot.key, second.snapshot.key)
        assertEquals(12L, second.snapshot.createdAtMillis)
        assertEquals(1L, second.snapshot.packetsSeen)
        assertEquals(TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.State.SYN_SENT, second.snapshot.state)
    }

    @Test
    fun delayedObservationDoesNotMoveLastSeenTimestampBackwards() {
        val tracker = TcpFlowLifecycleTracker()
        tracker.observe(
            parse(tcpPacket(flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_SYN)),
            TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.OUTBOUND, 20
        )
        val result = tracker.observe(
            parse(tcpPacket(flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_ACK)),
            TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.OUTBOUND, 10
        ) as TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.ObserveResult.Updated
        assertEquals(20L, result.snapshot.lastSeenAtMillis)
    }

    @Test
    fun enforcesCapacityAndRejectsUnsolicitedInitialAck() {
        val tracker = TcpFlowLifecycleTracker(maxFlows = 1)
        val ack = parse(tcpPacket(flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_ACK))
        assertEquals(
            TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.ObserveResult.InvalidInitialPacket,
            tracker.observe(ack, TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.OUTBOUND, 1)
        )

        val syn = parse(tcpPacket(flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_SYN))
        tracker.observe(syn, TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.OUTBOUND, 2)
        val secondSyn = parse(tcpPacket(
            sourcePort = 43121,
            flags = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.FLAG_SYN
        ))
        assertEquals(
            TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.ObserveResult.CapacityReached,
            tracker.observe(secondSyn, TcpFlowLifecycleTracker.Direction.OUTBOUND, 3)
        )
    }

    private fun parse(bytes: ByteArray) = Ipv4TcpPacketParser.parse(bytes)

    private fun tcpPacket(
        outbound: Boolean = true,
        sourcePort: Int = 43120,
        flags: Int
    ): ByteArray {
        val bytes = ByteArray(40)
        bytes[0] = 0x45
        put16(bytes, 2, bytes.size)
        put16(bytes, 4, 0x1234)
        bytes[8] = 64
        bytes[9] = 6
        val local = byteArrayOf(10, 0, 0, 2)
        val remote = byteArrayOf(203.toByte(), 0, 113, 10)
        (if (outbound) local else remote).copyInto(bytes, 12)
        (if (outbound) remote else local).copyInto(bytes, 16)

        val tcp = 20
        put16(bytes, tcp, if (outbound) sourcePort else 443)
        put16(bytes, tcp + 2, if (outbound) 443 else sourcePort)
        put32(bytes, tcp + 4, 100L)
        put32(bytes, tcp + 8, 200L)
        bytes[tcp + 12] = ((5 shl 4) or ((flags ushr 8) and 1)).toByte()
        bytes[tcp + 13] = flags.toByte()
        put16(bytes, tcp + 14, 4096)
        put16(bytes, tcp + 16, tcpChecksum(bytes, tcp, 20))
        fixIpChecksum(bytes)
        return bytes
    }

    private fun tcpChecksum(bytes: ByteArray, offset: Int, length: Int): Int {
        var sum = 0L
        for (i in 12 until 20 step 2) {
            sum += ((bytes[i].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or (bytes[i + 1].toInt() and 0xff)
        }
        sum += 6
        sum += length
        for (i in offset until offset + length step 2) {
            sum += ((bytes[i].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or (bytes[i + 1].toInt() and 0xff)
        }
        while ((sum ushr 16) != 0L) sum = (sum and 0xffff) + (sum ushr 16)
        return sum.toInt().inv() and 0xffff
    }

    private fun fixIpChecksum(bytes: ByteArray) {
        bytes[10] = 0
        bytes[11] = 0
        var sum = 0
        for (i in 0 until 20 step 2) {
            sum += ((bytes[i].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or (bytes[i + 1].toInt() and 0xff)
        }
        while ((sum ushr 16) != 0) sum = (sum and 0xffff) + (sum ushr 16)
        put16(bytes, 10, sum.inv() and 0xffff)
    }

    private fun put16(bytes: ByteArray, offset: Int, value: Int) {
        bytes[offset] = (value ushr 8).toByte()
        bytes[offset + 1] = value.toByte()
    }

    private fun put32(bytes: ByteArray, offset: Int, value: Long) {
        put16(bytes, offset, (value ushr 16).toInt() and 0xffff)
        put16(bytes, offset + 2, value.toInt() and 0xffff)
    }
}

package com.aboezzat.vpn.vpn

import com.aboezzat.vpn.proxy.ProxyProfile
import com.aboezzat.vpn.proxy.ProxyProtocol
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class Tun2ProxyCommandBuilderTest {
    @Test
    fun buildsHttpConnectCommandWithOwnedTunDescriptor() {
        val profile = ProxyProfile(
            name = "HTTP upstream",
            host = "proxy.example",
            port = 8080,
            protocol = ProxyProtocol.HTTP_CONNECT
        )

        val command = Tun2ProxyCommandBuilder.build(profile, tunFd = 42)

        assertTrue(command!!.startsWith("'tun2proxy-bin' "))
        assertTrue(command.contains("'--tun-fd' '42'"))
        assertTrue(command.contains("'--close-fd-on-drop' 'false'"))
        assertTrue(command.contains("'--proxy' 'http://proxy.example:8080'"))
        assertTrue(command.contains("'--dns' 'virtual'"))
        assertTrue(command.contains("'--ipv6-enabled'"))
    }

    @Test
    fun percentEncodesCredentialsAndBracketsIpv6ProxyHost() {
        val profile = ProxyProfile(
            name = "SOCKS upstream",
            host = "2001:db8::10",
            port = 1080,
            protocol = ProxyProtocol.SOCKS5,
            username = "user name",
            password = "p@ss:/#?"
        )

        val command = Tun2ProxyCommandBuilder.build(profile, tunFd = 9)

        assertTrue(command!!.contains("socks5://user%20name:p%40ss%3A%2F%23%3F@[2001:db8::10]:1080"))
        assertTrue(!command.contains("p@ss:/#?"))
    }

    @Test
    fun usesTcpDnsForwardingWhenVirtualRemoteDnsIsDisabled() {
        val profile = ProxyProfile(
            name = "HTTP",
            host = "proxy.example",
            port = 8080,
            protocol = ProxyProtocol.HTTP_CONNECT,
            remoteDns = false
        )

        val command = Tun2ProxyCommandBuilder.build(profile, tunFd = 8)

        assertTrue(command!!.contains("'--dns' 'over-tcp'"))
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsInvalidDescriptorAndMtu() {
        val profile = ProxyProfile(
            name = "SOCKS",
            host = "127.0.0.1",
            port = 1080,
            protocol = ProxyProtocol.SOCKS5
        )

        assertNull(Tun2ProxyCommandBuilder.build(profile, tunFd = -1))
        assertNull(Tun2ProxyCommandBuilder.build(profile, tunFd = 3, mtu = 500))
        assertNull(Tun2ProxyCommandBuilder.build(profile, tunFd = 3, mtu = 65536))
    }
}

# App-owned JNI bridge contract

Status: JNI callback bridge source and CMake target are now present in the app. The patched tun2proxy engine is not yet linked or shipped.

This document defines the Android socket-protection interface for the pinned tun2proxy fork. It does not establish a TUN or advertise a working VPN.

## Java/Kotlin ownership

- AboEzzatVpnService owns the ParcelFileDescriptor returned by VpnService.Builder.establish().
- The service exposes one synchronous method to native code: protectNativeSocket(fd: Int): Boolean.
- That method calls VpnService.protect(fd) directly. It must not dispatch protection to a Handler, coroutine, or executor.
- The service must not close the TUN descriptor until the native run call has returned.
- The native bridge must not cache an Activity. It currently holds a JNI global reference to the live service while the Android service is alive; this must be narrowed to the native run lifetime when the engine is integrated.

## Proposed C ABI

The fork should expose a callback registration API with explicit user data:

~~~c
typedef bool (*abz_socket_protect_fn)(int fd, void *user_data);

int tun2proxy_set_socket_protector(
    abz_socket_protect_fn callback,
    void *user_data
);

int tun2proxy_run_with_cli_args(
    const char *cli_args,
    unsigned short tun_mtu,
    bool close_fd_on_drop
);

int tun2proxy_stop(void);
~~~

Rules:

1. Registration is accepted only while no run is active.
2. Android tunnel mode refuses to start if the callback is absent.
3. The callback runs synchronously on the socket-creating thread and before connect, bind, send, or DNS network use.
4. A false callback result closes the socket and returns an error. No unprotected retry is permitted.
5. Callback and user data remain valid until the run function returns.
6. Stop is idempotent. A new run cannot start until the prior run exits and callback state is cleared.

The upstream run/stop functions alone are insufficient. The pinned fork must add this callback API and wire it into every Android outbound socket path.

## JNI callback mechanics

The current JNI bridge implements the following mechanics:

- Captures JavaVM once from JNI_OnLoad.
- Creates a global reference to the current service when the Android service starts.
- Caches the method ID for protectNativeSocket(I)Z while a valid JNI environment is available.
- In the native callback, calls GetEnv; attaches the current native thread only if detached; invokes the Java method synchronously; treats a Java exception as protection failure; detaches only if this callback attached the thread.
- Serializes callback-state teardown with an in-flight counter so the global reference cannot be deleted while a callback is using it.
- Clears the global reference only after callbacks in flight have returned. Native run integration still needs to enforce stop/run ordering.
- Treat missing service binding, JNI attach failure, a Java exception, or a failed JNI call as protection failure.

Do not hold a global lifecycle mutex while invoking Java. Use a short-lived state guard or in-flight callback counter to prevent stop/reference-deletion races without deadlocking Java error handling.

## Native socket factory requirements

At the pinned upstream revision, the central call sites are create_tcp_stream(...) and create_udp_stream(...) in src/lib.rs.

- TCP must be created unconnected (TcpSocket::new_v4/new_v6), protected by raw fd, then connected.
- UDP must be created unbound, protected by raw fd, then bound/connected and converted to Tokio.
- Protect the proxy endpoint and any UDP gateway endpoint as well as per-destination sockets.
- Resolve a proxy hostname through the underlying Android Network before TUN establishment, or require a numeric IP for the first milestone.
- Audit dependencies and optional features for independent outbound socket creation. Central call-site changes are not sufficient if another path creates sockets independently.

## Required tests before enabling the feature

### JNI/lifecycle tests

- Callback true: native socket creation proceeds.
- Callback false: socket closes and no connect/bind/send occurs.
- Missing callback: Android tunnel startup fails closed.
- Callback exception or JNI attach failure: fail closed.
- Stop during an in-flight callback: no use-after-free and no stale service reference.
- Stop waits for native run completion before Java closes the TUN descriptor.
- Repeated start/stop cycles do not retain global references or run threads.

### Socket tests

- IPv4 and IPv6 TCP sockets invoke protection exactly once before connect.
- IPv4 and IPv6 UDP sockets invoke protection exactly once before bind/connect.
- Proxy DNS and optional UDP gateway sockets are covered.
- A false result never falls back to an unprotected socket.

### Android device tests

Use a controlled proxy on the other phone. Verify its endpoint remains reachable while TUN is active. Repeat with Wi-Fi/mobile handover, VPN revoke, process stop, and a failed protect callback. Capture logs proving callback-before-connect ordering; do not infer it from a successful page load alone.

## Feature gate

Until the patched native library, JNI bridge, and tests above exist:

- Do not call Builder.establish() for production use.
- Do not label the app as connected or claim device-wide traffic protection.
- Keep saved per-app route choices as configuration only.
